1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any stratigraphy module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 500. The projective resolution has length at most 797 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to stratigraphy theory, we decompose the problem into 500 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 797. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the stratigraphy structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 494. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 384 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of structural decomposition and the invariant on the space spanned by stratigraphy generators. At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 384. QED.
